FROM "Orders" AS o
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT c."Id", c."CreatedAt", c."CreatedBy", c."CurrentBalance", c."CurrentLatitude", c."CurrentLongitude", c."Email", c."FirstName", c."IsAvailable", c."IsDeleted", c."IsOnline", c."LastLocationUpdate", c."LastName", c."LicensePlate", c."MerchantId", c."PasswordHash", c."PhoneNumber", c."UpdatedAt", c."UpdatedBy", c."VehicleBrand", c."VehicleModel", c."VehicleType", c.xmin
    FROM "Couriers" AS c
    WHERE NOT (c."IsDeleted")
) AS t ON o."CourierId" = t."Id"
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT m."Id", m."Address", m."CourierCompanyId", m."CourierCutFee", m."CreatedAt", m."CreatedBy", m."CrossRestaurantDistanceMeters", m."DefaultPackageFee", m."DispatchMode", m."Email", m."HexagonSizeMeters", m."IsActive", m."IsDeleted", m."IsOpen", m."Latitude", m."Longitude", m."MaxCourierDistanceKm", m."MaxOrdersPerTour", m."Name", m."OrderBatchingTimeMinutes", m."PasswordHash", m."PhoneNumber", m."ReconciliationPeriod", m."Role", m."UpdatedAt", m."UpdatedBy"
    FROM "Merchants" AS m
    WHERE NOT (m."IsDeleted")
) AS t0 ON o."MerchantId" = t0."Id"
WHERE NOT (o."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY o."CreatedAt" DESC
